# Seven boundary points: the complete AB/EF corner-line class

Consider the single-exterior N7 placement of N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. If the
line joining the two heptagon-interior points crosses the two hull edges
AB and EF, then its minimum ordinary triangle area is strictly below A*.
This is a complete exclusion of this line position, at every height and
for every orientation of the four oblique triangles.

The all-good oblique signs use the sharp projected theorem in
N7_PROJECTED_SIGNS.md. The other fifteen sign words are covered by eighteen
fixed cubic sums with 5,641 printed positive integer terms. Every
factor has a geometric justification below; the optional standard-library
checker `check_n7_corner_line.py` reads the tables and rebuilds the exact
arithmetic. No search or unprinted enumeration is a premise.

All determinants and polygon areas below are doubled. Put s=93/1000 and
K=149/20<Gamma2=4+2sqrt(3). Since s/2<A*, it suffices to treat
configurations whose actual unsigned doubled triangle areas are at least s.

## 1. The forced quadrilateral and the two separated diagonals

Use the strict convex actual hull

    A=(0,a), B=(b,0), C=(c,0), D=(1,d),
    E=(1,e), F=(f,1), G=(g,z), H=(0,h)

and the two heptagon-interior points O=(x,y), P=(u,v), with

    0<a<h<1, 0<b<c<1, 0<d<e<1,
    b<=f<=c, d<=h, a<=e, 0<g<f, h<z<1.

G lies strictly above HF, and every cyclic hull determinant is at least s.
Orient OP from its intersection with AB toward its intersection with EF.
Every point of the open edge EF has both coordinates strictly larger than
those of every point of the open edge AB, because f>=b and e>=a.
Consequently

    U=u-x>0, V=v-y>0,
    AOP,FOP,GOP,HOP>0; BOP,COP,DOP,EOP<0.             (1)

The entire chord between the two edge intersections lies in the actual
quadrilateral ABEF. Hence O,P both lie strictly inside that quadrilateral.
N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md proves that ABE,AEF,ABF,BEF each has area less
than 69/100<Gamma2*s, so each has capacity one. Both diagonals AE and BF
therefore strictly separate O,P. The identity

    IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP

fixes their orientations:

    AEO<0<AEP,             BFO>0>BFP.               (2)

Thus O belongs to ABE and ABF, and P belongs to AEF and BEF. In particular
BGP<0 by convexity: both E,F are strictly on the negative side of BG.
For either T=O or T=P, with ordinate y_T, the identities

    z BHT=h BGT-y_T[b(z-h)+hg],
    DFT=(1-d)CFT+[1-c+d(c-f)](1-y_T)                (3)

give BGT<0 => BHT<0 and CFT>0 => DFT>0. All displayed coefficients have
the required positive signs by the strict side orders and c>=f. In
particular BHP<0 is common to every row.

The positive primary OP differences are

    V-(e-a)U=AOP-EOP>=2s,
    U-(f-b)V=FOP-BOP>=2s.                            (4)

Since U,V>0 and e-a,f-b>=0, they imply U,V>=2s. The doubled area of ABEF is

    W0=1-(e-a)(f-b)<=1.

Its two diagonals separate O,P, so QUADRILATERAL_SPREAD.md applies with
normalized threshold s/W0>=s>1/11. Expressing P-O in the diagonal basis
(E-A,F-B) gives the two nonnegative factors

    S_AE=W0-6s-[V-(e-a)U],
    S_BF=W0-6s-[U-(f-b)V].                           (5)

Both bracketed quantities are positive by (4). No height comparison
between h and e is used here.

## 2. Exhaustive oblique-sign catalogue and its geometric consequences

If the oblique signs are

    ADO>0, BGO<0, CFP>0, EHP>0,                      (6)

then (1), U,V>0, and FGH>0 give precisely the hypotheses of
N7_PROJECTED_SIGNS.md. That theorem proves m<A* throughout this chamber.
Every other one of the sixteen sign words occurs in the following table.
Three words are split further by BHO; its two strict signs are exhaustive
because B,H,O are actual points and their triangle area is positive.

The additional signs for each row are in the next table; the row's
oblique and possible BHO choices are included. They follow from the
following finite geometric rules, without any numeric sign sampling.

1. Every triangle of the eight hull vertices in their cyclic order is
   positive. O,P lie inside the heptagon ABCDEFH and inside ABEF, so
   their oriented edge tests for either polygon are positive. Use (2)
   and BHP<0 from (3) as well.
2. Each four-consecutive-vertex cap has capacity one by
   N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. For such a cap beginning at I and ending at J,
   its interior is the negative side of IJ. If IOP<0<JOP, the IJ
   determinant decreases along OP, and IJO must be positive; otherwise
   both points would be in the cap. If IOP>0>JOP, the same argument
   forces IJP positive.
3. For any hull chord IJ with opposite OP endpoint signs, the exact
   difference IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP propagates an already known sign in its
   increasing or decreasing direction.
4. If three positive edge tests put T inside an actual hull triangle,
   every chord half-plane containing that triangle contains T. This
   propagates only signs justified by convexity of a stated triangle.
5. Every consecutive triple, every consecutive four-vertex cap, and
   ABE,ABF,AEF,BEF has capacity one. If one interior point is in such a
   polygon and all but one edge tests for the other point are positive,
   the remaining edge test is negative. The two implications in (3)
   are also used for either point.

Every sign used by a polynomial factor appears either in the common table
or in its row's additional table. A positive actual sign yields a factor
XYZ-s>=0; a negative sign yields -XYZ-s>=0. The checker independently
rebuilds these two tables by the five displayed rules.

## 3. Common rational box

Define

    eta=93/790, ell=7347/69700, Fmax=1-ell,
    h0=4s/Fmax, h1=1-s, f0=4s/(1-eta),
    x0=s/(h1-eta).

Use the following closed box in the coordinate order
(a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v):

| Coordinate | lower | upper |
|---|---|---|
| a | eta | 1-4s |
| b | x0 | Fmax |
| c | ell | Fmax |
| d | eta | h1 |
| e | h0 | 1 |
| f | f0 | Fmax |
| g | x0 | 3/5 |
| h | h0 | h1 |
| z | h0+s | 1 |
| x | x0 | Fmax-2s |
| y | eta | 1-3s |
| u | x0+2s | Fmax |
| v | eta+2s | 1-s |

The eight-hull-coordinate ranges are from N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md, with the
stronger g<3/5 proved in N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md Section 2. For T=O,P,
AHT=-(h-a)x_T and h-a<=h1-eta give x_T>=x0; the initial upper abscissa
bound is Fmax. N7_PLACEMENTS.md gives y_T>=eta. The two actual positive
edge tests FHT,EFT give the identity

    (1-e)FHT+(1-h)EFT=EFH(1-y_T).

Since EFH=(1-f)(1-h)+f(1-e)<=2-e-h, it follows that 1-y_T>=s.
Finally U,V>=2s gives the four tighter x,y,u,v endpoints in the table.
All endpoints are positive, and each lower endpoint is strictly below
its upper endpoint.

Let L_(2j)=coordinate_j-l_j and L_(2j+1)=u_j-coordinate_j for j=0,...,12.
The remaining affine factors are listed in their exact index order:

| Index | Factor | Index | Factor |
|---:|---|---:|---|
| 26 | h-a-s | 27 | 697/790-h+a |
| 28 | e-d-s | 29 | 697/790-e+d |
| 30 | c-b-117/1000 | 31 | 79/100-c+b |
| 32 | f-b | 33 | c-f |
| 34 | f-g | 35 | h-d |
| 36 | e-a | 37 | z-h-s |
| 38 | u-x-2s | 39 | v-y-2s |

These are all nonnegative by normalization, N7_PLACEMENTS.md, and (4).

## 4. Other nonnegative factors and their actual witnesses

The common support and cap factors are

    EP=f(1-a)-4s, EL=hc-4s, ER=e(1-b)-4s,
    SUP=f^2(z-a)-fg(1-a)-4s(f-g),
    P_AHGFE=f(1-e)+(1-f)(1-a-11s),
    P_BCDEF=e(1-f)+(1-e)(1-b-11s),
    P_DEFGH=g(h-d)+z-h-11sg,
    P_HABCD=(h-11s)(1-c)+dc,
    P_ABCDE=(e-11s)b+a(1-b),
    UABCD=8s-area(ABCD), UCDEF=8s-area(CDEF),
    UGHAB=8s-area(GHAB),
    UAEF=69/100-AEF, UABE=69/100-ABE.

The support bounds are proved in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md; its metric side-cap
bounds give the U factors for quadrilaterals. N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md
gives the two listed triangle upper bounds. The two strong spread factors
S_AE,S_BF are (5).

For an actual triangle T containing one or both of O,P, the symbols
`CT_1` and `CT_2` mean area(T)-3s and area(T)-Ks respectively. The first
comes from the three actual fan triangles; the second follows from the
Gamma2 bound with K<Gamma2. For an actual convex quadrilateral Q
containing both points, `Qabcd` means area(Q)-8s. Its nonnegativity is
the two-interior-point quadrilateral theorem of N8_OP_LINE_CAPS.md.

If both diagonals of that quadrilateral also separate O,P, the symbol
`Rabcd_IJ`, for one diagonal IJ, means

    area(abcd)-(29/5)s-epsilon*(IJO-IJP),             (7)

where epsilon is the sign of IJO in the row. QUADRILATERAL_SPREAD.md
proves the threshold-free constant 3+2sqrt(2)>29/5. The last term is
the positive actual spread across the specified diagonal, so (7) is
nonnegative. These R factors do not require the quadrilateral's area
to be at most one.

Finally `ELK` means hc-Ks. Its row contains an actual point in ABC or
ABH, as recorded below. That ear has area at least 3s, while the other
ear has area at least s. The paired-ear inequality

    sqrt(ABC)+sqrt(ABH)<=sqrt(hc)

therefore gives hc>=(sqrt(3s)+sqrt(s))^2=Gamma2*s>Ks.

Here are all occupancy and diagonal-separation witnesses used in the
printed products. The triangle entries name their actual occupants;
quadrilateral entries contain both O,P. For each spread entry both
hull diagonals separate O,P, and its suffix is the chosen diagonal.
Every required edge sign is in Section 2's tables.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | CABF_1: O; CABG_1: O; CABH_1: O; CADE_1: O | none | ABEF/AE | ABH: O |
| R2 | CBEH_2: OP | QADFH | none | none |
| R3 | CABG_1: O; CEFH_1: P | QABCF | none | none |
| R4 | none | none | none | none |
| R5 | none | none | none | none |
| R6 | none | QBDFH | none | none |
| R7 | none | QADEF | ACEF/AE, ACEF/CF | none |
| R8 | CCEF_1: P; CEFH_1: P | QBEFG | BEFH/EH | none |
| R9 | CABD_1: O; CABF_1: O; CABH_1: O; CBCF_1: P | none | ABDF/AD, ABEH/AE, ABEH/BH | ABH: O |
| R10 | CBEH_2: OP | none | ABDF/BF | none |
| R11 | CABD_1: O; CABG_1: O | QABCF | none | none |
| R12 | none | none | ABEG/BG | none |
| R13 | CABD_1: O; CABH_1: O; CEFH_1: P | none | none | ABH: O |

## 5. Exact contradictions and scope of the conclusion

For each row, let W be its printed sum of positive integer multiples of
nonnegative factors. Then W>=0. Expand it as sum(c_alpha X^alpha).
Using the common positive rational box gives the exact upper bound

    B(W)=sum_(c_alpha>0) c_alpha product_j(u_j^alpha_j)
         +sum_(c_alpha<0) c_alpha product_j(l_j^alpha_j).

Every value below is negative, contradicting W>=0. The eighteen rows
exhaust the fifteen oblique words other than (6). The remaining word
has the sharp strict bound m<A* from N7_PROJECTED_SIGNS.md. Hence the
entire AB/EF line class satisfies m<A*.

This theorem concerns the AB/EF edge pair in the literal seven-contact
normalization. It does not identify the other three opposite edge pairs
by a symmetry that would move the missing top contact. Their coverage,
and the all-interior N7 placement, remain separate global obligations.
